================ VLAN FILTER TEST (ETH_MACVTR VL / ETV / VTIM / VTHM / ERSVLM / ERIVLT / DOVLTC, ETH_MACPFR.VTFE) ================
  The CPU writes the tag(s) into the frame (VTIR = none, no context): the transmitter copies them, the receiver
  compares them with ETH_MACVTR. expect/actual: PASS = a frame came back, DROP = none within 5000 us.
  LT (RDES3): 1 = type, 4 = one tag, 5 = two tags. VF (RDES2, p.3068): 1 = passed the VLAN filter.
  RDES0: outer tag [15:0], inner tag [31:16] (only with stripping). MMC rx = ETH_RX_UNICAST_PACKETS_GOOD
  increment: +1 even for a dropped frame, the MMC counts before the VLAN filter (board 2026-11-12).
  Each case: description, setup, expectation -> [FLOW lines] -> result line -> frame sent / received.
  case B prints the [10]/[11] FLOW lines. VLAN hash (case H): software index of VID 200 = 0, VID 300 = 6
=======================================================================================

================ VLAN STRIP TEST (ETH_MACVTR EVLS / EIVLS, RDES0 OVT / IVT, RDES2.VF; VTFE = 0) ================
  The CPU writes the tag(s) into the frame (VTIR = none, no context): the transmitter copies them, the receiver
  compares them with ETH_MACVTR. expect/actual: PASS = a frame came back, DROP = none within 5000 us.
  LT (RDES3): 1 = type, 4 = one tag, 5 = two tags. VF (RDES2, p.3068): 1 = passed the VLAN filter.
  RDES0: outer tag [15:0], inner tag [31:16] (only with stripping). MMC rx = ETH_RX_UNICAST_PACKETS_GOOD
  increment: +1 even for a dropped frame, the MMC counts before the VLAN filter (board 2026-11-12).
  Each case: description, setup, expectation -> [FLOW lines] -> result line -> frame sent / received.
  case A prints the [10]/[11] FLOW lines.

================ VLAN STRIP TEST (ETH_MACVTR EVLS / EIVLS, RDES0 OVT / IVT, RDES2.VF; VTFE = 0) ================
  The CPU writes the tag(s) into the frame (VTIR = none, no context): the transmitter copies them, the receiver
  compares them with ETH_MACVTR. expect/actual: PASS = a frame came back, DROP = none within 5000 us.
  LT (RDES3): 1 = type, 4 = one tag, 5 = two tags. VF (RDES2, p.3068): 1 = passed the VLAN filter.
  RDES0: outer tag [15:0], inner tag [31:16] (only with stripping). MMC rx = ETH_RX_UNICAST_PACKETS_GOOD
  increment: +1 even for a dropped frame, the MMC counts before the VLAN filter (board 2026-11-12).
  Each case: description, setup, expectation -> [FLOW lines] -> result line -> frame sent / received.
  case A prints the [10]/[11] FLOW lines.

  --- case A: EVLS = always: the tag is removed, the frame is 4 bytes shorter, RDES0 keeps the tag value
      setup : MACPFR.VTFE = 0 (never drop, only report VF); MACVTR: VL = 0x0000 (VID 0), ETV = 0 (16-bit tag), VTIM = 0, VTHM = 0,
              ERSVLM = 0 (match C-tag), ERIVLT = 0 (outer tag), DOVLTC = 0, EDVLP = 1, EVLS = always, EIVLS = none

================ VLAN STRIP TEST (ETH_MACVTR EVLS / EIVLS, RDES0 OVT / IVT, RDES2.VF; VTFE = 0) ================
  The CPU writes the tag(s) into the frame (VTIR = none, no context): the transmitter copies them, the receiver
  compares them with ETH_MACVTR. expect/actual: PASS = a frame came back, DROP = none within 5000 us.
  LT (RDES3): 1 = type, 4 = one tag, 5 = two tags. VF (RDES2, p.3068): 1 = passed the VLAN filter.
  RDES0: outer tag [15:0], inner tag [31:16] (only with stripping). MMC rx = ETH_RX_UNICAST_PACKETS_GOOD
  increment: +1 even for a dropped frame, the MMC counts before the VLAN filter (board 2026-11-12).
  Each case: description, setup, expectation -> [FLOW lines] -> result line -> frame sent / received.
  case A prints the [10]/[11] FLOW lines.

  --- case A: EVLS = always: the tag is removed, the frame is 4 bytes shorter, RDES0 keeps the tag value
      setup : MACPFR.VTFE = 0 (never drop, only report VF); MACVTR: VL = 0x0000 (VID 0), ETV = 0 (16-bit tag), VTIM = 0, VTHM = 0,
              ERSVLM = 0 (match C-tag), ERIVLT = 0 (outer tag), DOVLTC = 0, EDVLP = 1, EVLS = always, EIVLS = none
      Expect: PASS, 60 bytes (tag bytes stripped), bytes 12..13 = 88 B5, RDES0 [15:0] = 0x00000064

        -> PR=1 HUC=0 HMC=0 DAIF=0 PM=0 DBF=0 PCF=0 SAIF=0 SAF=0 HPF=0 RA=0
